`src/server/domain/pro/leave.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { and, asc, desc, eq, gte, inArray, isNotNull, isNull, lt } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { agendaBlocks, alerts, appointments, auditLog, professionals } from "../../db/schema";
import { UserFacingError } from "../errors";
import { emit } from "../events";
import { assertCanBook } from "../agenda/mode";
import { periodLabel, validateLeave, type LeaveInput } from "@/lib/leave-request";
import { folgaAlertKey } from "@/lib/alert-links";
import { blockAppliesOn } from "@/lib/agenda-blocks";
import { dayOf, hmToMinutes, shiftDay, todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { slotMs } from "@/lib/schedule";
import { firstName } from "@/lib/authorship";
import { minuteIn, timeOf, titleCase } from "@/lib/pro-day";
import { queueLeaveNotificationTx } from "./notifications";
import type { ProContext } from "../../queries/pro";

export type LeaveRequestItem = { id: string; professionalId: string; professional: string; title: string; period: string; notes: string | null; requestedAt: string; clients: string[] };
export type MyLeaveItem = { id: string; title: string; period: string; status: "pedido" | "aprovado" | "recusado"; note: string | null };
type BlockShape = { professionalId: string | null; startsOn: string; endsOn: string; startTime: string; endTime: string; weekdays: number[] | null };

const OCCUPY = ["agendado", "confirmado", "chegou", "em_atendimento", "solicitado"];

/** Clientes marcadas da profissional dentro do período, para a recepção remarcar ("Paula 15:00 (20/10)"). Nada é remarcado sozinho. */
async function clientsInPeriod(b: BlockShape): Promise<string[]> {
  if (!b.professionalId) return [];
  const s = hmToMinutes(b.startTime) ?? 0, e = hmToMinutes(b.endTime) ?? 1440;
  const rows = await db.select({ startsAt: appointments.startsAt, name: appointments.clientName }).from(appointments)
    .where(and(eq(appointments.professionalId, b.professionalId), inArray(appointments.status, OCCUPY), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf),
      gte(appointments.startsAt, new Date(slotMs(b.startsOn, "00:00"))), lt(appointments.startsAt, new Date(slotMs(shiftDay(b.endsOn, 1), "00:00")))))
    .orderBy(asc(appointments.startsAt));
  return rows.filter((r) => { const m = minuteIn(r.startsAt.getTime()); return blockAppliesOn(b, dayOf(r.startsAt)) && m >= s && m < e; })
    .map((r) => { const d = dayOf(r.startsAt); return `${titleCase(firstName(r.name) ?? "cliente")} ${timeOf(r.startsAt.getTime())} (${d.slice(8, 10)}/${d.slice(5, 7)})`; });
}

/** Pedido de folga ou bloqueio pelo app: nasce "pedido" (só avisa no motor), avisa a gestão no sino e vale em qualquer modo da agenda. */
export async function requestLeave(pro: ProContext, input: LeaveInput, now = new Date()): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  const v = validateLeave(input, todayBahia(now));
  if (!v.ok) throw new UserFacingError(v.error);
  const r = v.row;
  const id = randomUUID();
  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    await tx.insert(agendaBlocks).values({
      id, professionalId: pro.professionalId, title: r.title, notes: r.notes, startsOn: r.startsOn, endsOn: r.endsOn, startTime: r.startTime, endTime: r.endTime, weekdays: null,
      externalKey: `crm:blq:${id}`, origin: "crm", reason: r.reason, createdBy: pro.userId, requestStatus: "pedido", requestedBy: pro.userId, createdAt: now,
    });
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: pro.userId, action: "pediu_folga", entity: "agenda_block", entityId: id, data: { ...r } });
    await tx.insert(alerts).values({ userId: null, audience: "gestao", type: "folga_pedida", severity: "info", dedupeKey: folgaAlertKey(id),
      message: `${titleCase(pro.name)} pediu ${r.title.toLowerCase()} em ${periodLabel(r)}. Aprove ou recuse na Agenda.` });
  });
  await emit("agenda_alterada", { day: r.startsOn });
  await emit("alerta", {});
  return { id };
}

/**
 * Aprovar ou recusar (recepção, `agenda.marcar`). Aprovar é criar bloqueio: segue o modo da agenda (recusado no modo consulta).
 * Recusar exige o motivo, que ela vê. Os dois avisam a profissional e resolvem o aviso da gestão. Responder duas vezes é recusado.
 */
export async function decideLeave(blockId: string, decision: "aprovar" | "recusar", note: string | null, actorId: string, now = new Date()): Promise<{ clients: string[] }> {
  const text = note?.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ") ?? "";
  if (decision === "recusar" && text.length < 3) throw new UserFacingError("Escreva o motivo da recusa (ela vai ver).");
  if (text.length > 200) throw new UserFacingError("Use até 200 caracteres.");
  if (decision === "aprovar") await assertCanBook();
  const b = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const [row] = await tx.select().from(agendaBlocks).where(eq(agendaBlocks.id, blockId)).for("update");
    if (!row || row.deletedAt) throw new UserFacingError("Pedido não encontrado.");
    if (row.requestStatus !== "pedido") throw new UserFacingError("Esse pedido já foi respondido.");
    const status = decision === "aprovar" ? "aprovado" : "recusado";
    await tx.update(agendaBlocks).set({ requestStatus: status, decidedBy: actorId, decidedAt: now, decisionNote: text || null, updatedAt: now, updatedBy: actorId }).where(eq(agendaBlocks.id, blockId));
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: actorId, action: decision === "aprovar" ? "aprovou_folga" : "recusou_folga", entity: "agenda_block", entityId: blockId, data: { de: "pedido", para: status, nota: text || null } });
    await tx.update(alerts).set({ resolvedAt: now, readAt: now }).where(and(eq(alerts.dedupeKey, folgaAlertKey(blockId)), isNull(alerts.resolvedAt)));
    if (row.professionalId) await queueLeaveNotificationTx(tx, { professionalId: row.professionalId, blockId, approved: decision === "aprovar", period: periodLabel(row), note: text || null, actorId, now });
    return row;
  });
  await emit("agenda_alterada", { day: b.startsOn });
  await emit("alerta", {});
  return { clients: decision === "aprovar" ? await clientsInPeriod(b) : [] };
}

/** "Pedidos (n)" da Agenda: os pedidos esperando resposta, com quem está marcada no período. */
export async function listLeaveRequests(): Promise<LeaveRequestItem[]> {
  const rows = await db.select({ b: agendaBlocks, pro: professionals.name }).from(agendaBlocks).innerJoin(professionals, eq(professionals.id, agendaBlocks.professionalId))
    .where(and(eq(agendaBlocks.requestStatus, "pedido"), isNull(agendaBlocks.deletedAt))).orderBy(asc(agendaBlocks.startsOn), asc(agendaBlocks.createdAt));
  return Promise.all(rows.map(async ({ b, pro }) => ({
    id: b.id, professionalId: b.professionalId!, professional: titleCase(pro), title: b.title, period: periodLabel(b), notes: b.notes, requestedAt: b.createdAt.toISOString(), clients: await clientsInPeriod(b),
  })));
}

/** Os pedidos dela: aguardando, aprovados e recusados (com o motivo), dos últimos 30 dias para a frente. */
export async function listMyLeaves(pro: ProContext, now = new Date()): Promise<MyLeaveItem[]> {
  const rows = await db.select().from(agendaBlocks).where(and(eq(agendaBlocks.professionalId, pro.professionalId), isNotNull(agendaBlocks.requestStatus), isNull(agendaBlocks.deletedAt),
    gte(agendaBlocks.endsOn, shiftDay(todayBahia(now), -30)))).orderBy(desc(agendaBlocks.startsOn));
  return rows.map((b) => ({ id: b.id, title: b.title, period: periodLabel(b), status: b.requestStatus!, note: b.decisionNote }));
}
```

```ts
import { requestLeave } from "@/server/domain/pro/leave";

const leaveSchema = z.object({
  startsOn: z.string().max(10), endsOn: z.string().max(10), allDay: z.boolean(), startTime: z.string().max(5), endTime: z.string().max(5), reason: z.string().max(20), note: z.string().max(400),
});

/** Pedir folga ou bloqueio: fica "Aguardando a recepção". */
export async function requestLeaveAction(input: z.input<typeof leaveSchema>): Promise<ProResult> {
  try {
    const user = await assertPerm("pro.app");
    await requestLeave(await proOf(user), leaveSchema.parse(input));
    revalidatePath("/pro", "layout");
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (e) { return fail(e, "Não deu para pedir. Tente de novo."); }
}
```

`src/components/pro/leave-form.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Field, Input, Select } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { requestLeaveAction } from "@/app/(pro)/pro/actions";
import { LEAVE_REASONS } from "@/lib/leave-request";

/** Pedir folga ou bloqueio: dia ou período, das … às … e motivo. Quem decide é a recepção. */
export function LeaveForm({ today }: { today: string }) {
  const [v, setV] = useState({ startsOn: today, endsOn: today, allDay: true, startTime: "09:00", endTime: "18:00", reason: "folga", note: "" });
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const set = (patch: Partial<typeof v>) => setV((x) => ({ ...x, ...patch }));
  const submit = () => start(async () => {
    setError(null);
    const r = await requestLeaveAction(v);
    if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); return; }
    toast("Pedido enviado. Fica 'Aguardando a recepção'.");
    router.refresh();
  });
  return (
    <form noValidate className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper p-4" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); submit(); }}>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <Field label="Dia" htmlFor="folga-ini"><Input id="folga-ini" type="date" min={today} value={v.startsOn} onChange={(e) => set({ startsOn: e.target.value, endsOn: v.endsOn < e.target.value ? e.target.value : v.endsOn })} /></Field>
        <Field label="Até" htmlFor="folga-fim"><Input id="folga-fim" type="date" min={v.startsOn} value={v.endsOn} onChange={(e) => set({ endsOn: e.target.value })} /></Field>
      </div>
      <label className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" className="size-5" checked={v.allDay} onChange={(e) => set({ allDay: e.target.checked })} />Dia todo</label>
      {!v.allDay && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <Field label="Das" htmlFor="folga-das"><Input id="folga-das" type="time" step={900} value={v.startTime} onChange={(e) => set({ startTime: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Às" htmlFor="folga-as"><Input id="folga-as" type="time" step={900} value={v.endTime} onChange={(e) => set({ endTime: e.target.value })} /></Field>
        </div>
      )}
      <Field label="Motivo" htmlFor="folga-motivo">
        <Select id="folga-motivo" value={v.reason} onChange={(e) => set({ reason: e.target.value })}>
          {Object.entries(LEAVE_REASONS).map(([k, l]) => <option key={k} value={k}>{l}</option>)}
        </Select>
      </Field>
      <Field label={v.reason === "outro" ? "Qual o motivo?" : "Observação (opcional)"} htmlFor="folga-obs" error={error}>
        <Input id="folga-obs" value={v.note} maxLength={200} onChange={(e) => set({ note: e.target.value })} aria-invalid={!!error} />
      </Field>
      <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={pending}>Pedir</Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { requirePro } from "@/server/auth/pro-session";
import { listMyLeaves } from "@/server/domain/pro/leave";
import { todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { LeaveForm } from "@/components/pro/leave-form";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

export const metadata = { title: "Folga" };
const STATUS = { pedido: "Aguardando a recepção", aprovado: "Aprovada", recusado: "Recusada" } as const;

export default async function ProLeavePage() {
  const { pro } = await requirePro();
  if (!pro) return null;
  const mine = await listMyLeaves(pro);
  return (
    <main className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <h1 className="font-display text-[24px]">Pedir folga ou bloqueio</h1>
      <LeaveForm today={todayBahia(new Date())} />
      {mine.length > 0 && (
        <section aria-labelledby="meus-pedidos">
          <h2 id="meus-pedidos" className="mb-2 font-medium">Seus pedidos</h2>
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {mine.map((l) => (
              <li key={l.id} className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line px-3 py-2">
                <p>{l.title} · {l.period}</p>
                <p className={cn("text-sm", l.status === "recusado" ? "text-alert" : l.status === "aprovado" ? "text-ok" : "text-gold-ink")}>{STATUS[l.status]}{l.status === "recusado" && l.note ? `: ${l.note}` : ""}</p>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
```

```ts
"use server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { z } from "zod";
import { assertPerm } from "@/server/auth/session";
import { decideLeave } from "@/server/domain/pro/leave";
import { userErrorMessage } from "@/server/user-error";

export type ActionResult<T = undefined> = { ok: true; data?: T } | { ok: false; error: string };

/** Aprovar ou recusar um pedido de folga (aprovar segue o modo da agenda). */
export async function decideLeaveAction(blockId: string, decision: string, note: string): Promise<ActionResult<{ clients: string[] }>> {
  try {
    const user = await assertPerm("agenda.marcar");
    const r = await decideLeave(z.string().uuid().parse(blockId), z.enum(["aprovar", "recusar"]).parse(decision), z.string().max(400).parse(note), user.id);
    revalidatePath("/agenda");
    return { ok: true, data: r };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "folga", "Não deu para responder o pedido. Tente de novo.") }; }
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { decideLeaveAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/profissional-actions";
import type { LeaveRequestItem } from "@/server/domain/pro/leave";

/** "Pedidos (n)" na Agenda: os pedidos de folga das profissionais, com quem está marcada no período. Nada é remarcado sozinho. */
export function LeaveRequestsButton({ items, openInitially }: { items: LeaveRequestItem[]; openInitially: boolean }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(openInitially && items.length > 0);
  const [refusing, setRefusing] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  if (!items.length) return null;
  const decide = (id: string, d: "aprovar" | "recusar") => start(async () => {
    const r = await decideLeaveAction(id, d, d === "recusar" ? note : "");
    if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    toast(d === "recusar" ? "Recusado" : r.data?.clients.length ? `Aprovado. Remarque: ${r.data.clients.join(", ")}` : "Aprovado");
    setRefusing(null); setNote(""); router.refresh();
  });
  return (
    <>
      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>Pedidos ({items.length})</Button>
      <Sheet open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} title="Pedidos de folga">
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {items.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.id} className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line p-3">
              <p className="font-medium">{r.professional} · {r.title}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-graphite">{r.period}{r.notes ? ` · ${r.notes}` : ""}</p>
              {r.clients.length > 0 && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-alert">{r.clients.length === 1 ? "1 cliente marcada" : `${r.clients.length} clientes marcadas`}: {r.clients.join(", ")}. Remarque antes ou depois de aprovar.</p>}
              {refusing === r.id ? (
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <Input aria-label={`Motivo da recusa para ${r.professional}`} value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} placeholder="Motivo (ela vai ver)" maxLength={200} />
                  <div className="flex gap-2"><Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setRefusing(null)}>Voltar</Button><Button variant="danger" loading={pending} onClick={() => decide(r.id, "recusar")}>Recusar</Button></div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2"><Button loading={pending} onClick={() => decide(r.id, "aprovar")}>Aprovar</Button><Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => { setNote(""); setRefusing(r.id); }}>Recusar</Button></div>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Sheet>
    </>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { appointments, professionals, proNotifications, users } from "../../db/schema";
import { resetDb } from "../../../../test/db";
import { resolvePro, type ProContext } from "../../queries/pro";
import { ackDay, dayAcks } from "./acks";

const bahia = (day: string, hm: string) => new Date(`${day}T${hm}:00-03:00`);
let maiara: ProContext;
let apId = "";

beforeEach(async () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(bahia("2026-10-15", "19:02"));
  await resetDb();
  const [u] = await db.insert(users).values({ name: "Maiara Lima", email: "m@c.com", role: "atendente", passwordHash: "x" }).returning();
  await db.insert(professionals).values({ name: "MAIARA", userId: u.id });
  maiara = (await resolvePro(u.id))!;
  apId = (await db.insert(appointments).values({ clientName: "Paula", professionalId: maiara.professionalId, serviceLabel: "Corte", startsAt: bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"),
    status: "agendado", origin: "crm", externalKey: "t:1", updatedAt: bahia("2026-10-15", "12:00") }).returning())[0].id;
});
afterEach(() => { vi.useRealTimers(); });

describe("'Vi minha agenda de amanhã' (seção 8.4.5)", () => {
  it("grava a hora; sem mudança depois, não acusa nada", async () => {
    await ackDay(maiara, "2026-10-16");
    expect(await dayAcks("2026-10-16")).toEqual({ [maiara.professionalId]: { ackedAt: bahia("2026-10-15", "19:02").toISOString(), changedAfter: false } });
  });

  it("horário dela daquele dia mexido depois do 'vi' aparece como mudou", async () => {
    await ackDay(maiara, "2026-10-16");
    vi.setSystemTime(bahia("2026-10-15", "20:00"));
    await db.update(appointments).set({ notes: "trazer foto", updatedAt: new Date() }).where(eq(appointments.id, apId));
    expect((await dayAcks("2026-10-16"))[maiara.professionalId].changedAfter).toBe(true);
  });

  it("horário que saiu da agenda dela (aviso daquele dia) também conta; outro dia não", async () => {
    await ackDay(maiara, "2026-10-16");
    await db.insert(proNotifications).values({ userId: maiara.userId, professionalId: maiara.professionalId, kind: "remarcado", title: "Saiu da sua agenda", body: "x",
      url: "/pro/dia/2026-10-16", dedupeKey: "k1", createdAt: bahia("2026-10-15", "20:00") });
    expect((await dayAcks("2026-10-16"))[maiara.professionalId].changedAfter).toBe(true);
    expect(await dayAcks("2026-10-17")).toEqual({});
  });

  it("ver de novo atualiza a hora e zera o 'mudou'", async () => {
    await ackDay(maiara, "2026-10-16");
    vi.setSystemTime(bahia("2026-10-15", "20:00"));
    await db.update(appointments).set({ updatedAt: new Date() }).where(eq(appointments.id, apId));
    vi.setSystemTime(bahia("2026-10-15", "21:00"));
    await ackDay(maiara, "2026-10-16");
    expect((await dayAcks("2026-10-16"))[maiara.professionalId]).toEqual({ ackedAt: bahia("2026-10-15", "21:00").toISOString(), changedAfter: false });
  });
});
```

`src/server/domain/pro/acks.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { proDayAcks } from "../../db/schema";
import { shiftDay } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { slotMs } from "@/lib/schedule";
import type { ProContext } from "../../queries/pro";

/** "Vi minha agenda de amanhã": grava (ou atualiza) quando ela viu a agenda daquele dia. */
export async function ackDay(pro: ProContext, day: string, now = new Date()): Promise<void> {
  await db.insert(proDayAcks).values({ professionalId: pro.professionalId, day, ackedAt: now, ackedBy: pro.userId })
    .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: [proDayAcks.professionalId, proDayAcks.day], set: { ackedAt: now, ackedBy: pro.userId } });
}

export type DayAck = { ackedAt: string; changedAfter: boolean };

/**
 * Para a Agenda ("Ana viu a agenda de amanhã às 19:02") e para ela: quem viu o dia e se mudou depois.
 * "Mudou" junta dois sinais: um horário dela daquele dia com `updated_at` depois do "vi", ou um aviso dela daquele dia
 * (URL `/pro/dia/<dia>`) criado depois. O aviso é o que pega o horário que saiu da agenda dela.
 */
export async function dayAcks(day: string): Promise<Record<string, DayAck>> {
  const from = new Date(slotMs(day, "00:00")).toISOString(), to = new Date(slotMs(shiftDay(day, 1), "00:00")).toISOString();
  const r = await db.execute(sql`
    select k.professional_id as id, k.acked_at as "ackedAt",
      (exists (select 1 from appointments a where a.professional_id = k.professional_id and a.avec_twin_of is null
                 and a.starts_at >= ${from}::timestamptz and a.starts_at < ${to}::timestamptz and a.updated_at > k.acked_at)
       or exists (select 1 from pro_notifications n where n.professional_id = k.professional_id and n.created_at > k.acked_at
                 and n.url like ${`/pro/dia/${day}%`})) as "changedAfter"
    from pro_day_acks k where k.day = ${day}::date`);
  return Object.fromEntries((r.rows as { id: string; ackedAt: Date | string; changedAfter: boolean }[]).map((x) => [x.id, { ackedAt: new Date(x.ackedAt).toISOString(), changedAfter: x.changedAfter }]));
}
```

`src/server/queries/entry-extras.ts`:

```ts
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { appointments } from "../db/schema";
import { titleCase } from "@/lib/pro-day";
import { listClientNotes, type NoteView } from "./pro";

export type EntryExtras = { finishedBy: string | null; notes: NoteView[]; canNote: boolean };

/**
 * O que o card do horário mostra a mais (Lote 7A): "Finalizado por Ana", quando foi a profissional pelo app, e a ficha técnica da cliente.
 * `canNote` diz se dá para "Anotar para a profissional", o que exige a cliente ter ficha no CRM.
 */
export async function getEntryExtras(appointmentId: string): Promise<EntryExtras> {
  const [a] = await db.select({ contactId: appointments.contactId }).from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.id, appointmentId));
  if (!a) return { finishedBy: null, notes: [], canNote: false };
  const fin = await db.execute(sql`select p.name from audit_log l join professionals p on p.user_id = l.user_id
    where l.entity = 'appointment' and l.entity_id = ${appointmentId} and l.data->>'para' = 'compareceu' order by l.created_at desc limit 1`);
  const name = (fin.rows[0] as { name: string } | undefined)?.name;
  return { finishedBy: name ? titleCase(name) : null, notes: a.contactId ? await listClientNotes(a.contactId, 3) : [], canNote: !!a.contactId };
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { ackDayAction } from "@/app/(pro)/pro/actions";
import { timeOf } from "@/lib/pro-day";
import type { DayAck } from "@/server/domain/pro/acks";

/** "Vi minha agenda de amanhã" e, se mudou depois, o aviso para ela olhar de novo. */
export function AckButton({ day, ack }: { day: string; ack: DayAck | null }) {
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  if (ack && !ack.changedAfter) return <p role="status" className="text-center text-sm text-ok">Você viu a agenda de amanhã às {timeOf(Date.parse(ack.ackedAt))}.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      {ack?.changedAfter && <p role="status" className="text-center text-sm font-medium text-alert">A agenda mudou depois que você viu.</p>}
      <Button size="lg" loading={pending} onClick={() => start(async () => { const r = await ackDayAction(day); if (r.ok) router.refresh(); else toast(r.error, "error"); })}>
        Vi minha agenda de amanhã
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { DayAck } from "@/server/domain/pro/acks";
import { dueLabel } from "@/lib/next-action";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

/** No topo da coluna da profissional: "Viu ontem 19:02" ou "Mudou depois que ela viu". */
export function ProAckLine({ ack, nowMs }: { ack?: DayAck; nowMs: number }) {
  if (!ack) return null;
  return (
    <span className={cn("block truncate text-[11px] font-medium", ack.changedAfter ? "text-alert" : "text-ok")}>
      {ack.changedAfter ? "Mudou depois que ela viu" : `Viu ${dueLabel(Date.parse(ack.ackedAt), nowMs)}`}
    </span>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { getEntryExtras, type EntryExtras } from "@/server/queries/entry-extras";
import { addClientNote } from "@/server/domain/client-notes";

/** O que o card do horário mostra a mais: "Finalizado por Ana" e a ficha técnica. */
export async function entryExtrasAction(appointmentId: string): Promise<ActionResult<EntryExtras>> {
  try {
    await assertPerm("atendimento.ver");
    return { ok: true, data: await getEntryExtras(z.string().uuid().parse(appointmentId)) };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "agenda", "Não deu para carregar.") }; }
}

/** "Anotar para a profissional": vai para a ficha técnica e ela vê na próxima cliente. */
export async function addNoteForProAction(appointmentId: string, body: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  try {
    const user = await assertPerm("atendimento.ver");
    await addClientNote({ appointmentId: z.string().uuid().parse(appointmentId), body: z.string().max(4000).parse(body) }, { userId: user.id, professionalId: null });
    revalidatePath("/agenda");
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "agenda", "Não deu para salvar a anotação.") }; }
}
```

`src/components/agenda/entry-extras.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { addNoteForProAction, entryExtrasAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/profissional-actions";
import type { EntryExtras as Extras } from "@/server/queries/entry-extras";

/** No card do horário: "Finalizado por Ana", a ficha técnica e "Anotar para a profissional". Carrega ao abrir o card. */
export function EntryExtras({ appointmentId }: { appointmentId: string }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<Extras | null>(null);
  const [writing, setWriting] = useState(false);
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const load = () => entryExtrasAction(appointmentId).then((r) => { if (r.ok && r.data) setData(r.data); });
  useEffect(() => { let alive = true; entryExtrasAction(appointmentId).then((r) => { if (alive && r.ok && r.data) setData(r.data); }); return () => { alive = false; }; }, [appointmentId]);
  if (!data) return null;
  const save = () => start(async () => {
    const r = await addNoteForProAction(appointmentId, text);
    if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    toast("Anotado na ficha"); setText(""); setWriting(false); await load();
  });
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm">
      {data.finishedBy && <p className="text-graphite">Finalizado por {data.finishedBy}</p>}
      {data.notes.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {data.notes.map((n) => <li key={n.id} className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-paper-2 px-3 py-1.5">{n.body}{n.author ? <span className="text-graphite"> · {n.author}</span> : null}</li>)}
        </ul>
      )}
      {data.canNote && (writing ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Textarea aria-label="Anotar para a profissional" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} maxLength={2000} placeholder="Ela vê na próxima cliente (ex.: alérgica a amônia)" />
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2"><Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setWriting(false)}>Voltar</Button><Button loading={pending} disabled={!text.trim()} onClick={save}>Salvar</Button></div>
        </div>
      ) : <button type="button" className="self-start min-h-11 font-medium text-gold-ink underline-offset-4 hover:underline" onClick={() => setWriting(true)}>Anotar para a profissional</button>)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
};

    expect((await getProEarnings(maiara, NOW, { showServiceValue: false })).waiting).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`src/server/queries/pro-earnings.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { firstName } from "@/lib/authorship";
import { dayOf, todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { slotMs } from "@/lib/schedule";
import type { ProContext } from "./pro";

export type EarningLine = { id: string; day: string; client: string; label: string; pct: string | null; commissionCents: number | null; serviceCents: number | null; why: string | null };
export type EarningsPeriod = { totalCents: number; lines: EarningLine[] };
export type Earnings = { configured: boolean; today: EarningsPeriod; month: EarningsPeriod; waiting: { id: string; day: string; client: string; service: string }[] };

const pctText = (bp: number) => `${(bp / 100).toLocaleString("pt-BR", { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}%`;
const total = (lines: EarningLine[]) => lines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.commissionCents ?? 0), 0);

/**
 * Os ganhos dela no mês e hoje (seção 8.4.7): linhas de comanda fechadas e não canceladas com o nome dela (a comissão é o retrato do fechamento).
 * O valor do serviço só aparece com `mostraValorServico`; sem regra de comissão, a linha diz o que fazer.
 * Horário finalizado no CRM e ainda sem comanda fechada aparece como "aguardando fechamento". Nunca mostra o que é de outra profissional.
 */
export async function getProEarnings(pro: ProContext, now: Date, opts: { showServiceValue: boolean }): Promise<Earnings> {
  const today = todayBahia(now);
  const monthStart = `${today.slice(0, 8)}01`;
  const from = new Date(slotMs(monthStart, "00:00")).toISOString();
  const [conf, rows, waiting] = await Promise.all([
    db.execute(sql`select exists (select 1 from professional_services where professional_id = ${pro.professionalId} and commission_bp is not null) as ok`),
    db.execute(sql`select ci.id, c.day::text as day, coalesce(ct.name, c.client_name) as client, ci.label, ci.kind,
        ci.commission_bp as bp, ci.commission_base_cents as base, ci.commission_cents as cents, ci.total_cents as total
      from comanda_items ci join comandas c on c.id = ci.comanda_id left join contacts ct on ct.id = c.contact_id
      where ci.professional_id = ${pro.professionalId} and ci.cancelled_at is null and ci.kind in ('servico', 'pacote_sessao')
        and c.status = 'fechada' and c.cancelled_at is null and c.day >= ${monthStart}::date and c.day <= ${today}::date
      order by c.day desc, c.closed_at desc, ci.created_at`),
    db.execute(sql`select a.id, a.starts_at as at, a.client_name as client, a.service_label as service from appointments a
      where a.professional_id = ${pro.professionalId} and a.status = 'compareceu' and a.finished_at is not null and a.avec_twin_of is null
        and a.starts_at >= ${from}::timestamptz
        and not exists (select 1 from comanda_items ci join comandas c on c.id = ci.comanda_id
                        where ci.appointment_id = a.id and ci.cancelled_at is null and c.status = 'fechada')
      order by a.starts_at desc`),
  ]);
  type Row = { id: string; day: string; client: string | null; label: string; kind: string; bp: number | null; base: number | null; cents: number | null; total: number | null };
  const lines = (rows.rows as Row[]).map((r): EarningLine => ({
    id: r.id, day: r.day, client: firstName(r.client) ?? "Cliente", label: r.label, pct: r.bp != null ? pctText(r.bp) : null, commissionCents: r.cents,
    serviceCents: opts.showServiceValue ? r.total : null,
    why: r.bp == null ? `Comissão ainda não configurada para ${r.label}. Fale com a gestão.` : r.kind === "pacote_sessao" && r.base == null ? "Valor da sessão não definido. Fale com a gestão." : null,
  }));
  const todays = lines.filter((l) => l.day === today);
  return {
    configured: !!(conf.rows[0] as { ok: boolean } | undefined)?.ok,
    today: { totalCents: total(todays), lines: todays },
    month: { totalCents: total(lines), lines },
    waiting: (waiting.rows as { id: string; at: Date | string; client: string | null; service: string }[]).map((w) => ({ id: w.id, day: dayOf(new Date(w.at)), client: firstName(w.client) ?? "Cliente", service: w.service })),
  };
}
```

```tsx
import type { Earnings, EarningsPeriod } from "@/server/queries/pro-earnings";
import { formatBRL } from "@/lib/money";

function Period({ title, p }: { title: string; p: EarningsPeriod }) {
  return (
    <section aria-label={title} className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper p-4">
      <p className="text-sm text-graphite">{title}</p>
      <p className="font-display text-[30px] leading-tight tabular">{formatBRL(p.totalCents)}</p>
      {p.lines.length === 0 ? <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-graphite">Nada fechado ainda.</p> : (
        <ul className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1.5 text-sm">
          {p.lines.map((l) => (
            <li key={l.id}>
              {l.client} · {l.label}{l.serviceCents != null ? ` (${formatBRL(l.serviceCents)})` : ""}
              {l.why ? <span className="text-gold-ink"> · {l.why}</span> : <> · {l.pct} = <b className="font-semibold">{formatBRL(l.commissionCents ?? 0)}</b></>}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

/** Ganhos: hoje e o mês, com o total da comissão em destaque; "aguardando fechamento" para o que ainda não foi pago. */
export function EarningsView({ data }: { data: Earnings }) {
  if (!data.configured) return <p role="status" className="text-graphite">Comissão ainda não configurada. Fale com a gestão.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <Period title="Hoje" p={data.today} />
      <Period title="Este mês" p={data.month} />
      {data.waiting.length > 0 && (
        <section aria-label="Aguardando fechamento" className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-dashed border-line p-4 text-sm">
          <p className="font-medium">Aguardando fechamento</p>
          <ul className="mt-1 flex flex-col gap-1">{data.waiting.map((w) => <li key={w.id}>{w.day.slice(8, 10)}/{w.day.slice(5, 7)} · {w.client} · {w.service}</li>)}</ul>
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { requirePerm } from "@/server/auth/session";
import { resolvePro } from "@/server/queries/pro";
import { getProEarnings } from "@/server/queries/pro-earnings";
import { getProAppSettings } from "@/server/domain/pro/settings";
import { caixaOn } from "@/server/domain/agenda/ports";
import { EarningsView } from "@/components/pro/earnings-view";

export const metadata = { title: "Ganhos" };

/** Ganhos: exige `pro.ganhos` e o caixa no CRM (seção 8.4.7). */
export default async function ProEarningsPage() {
  const user = await requirePerm("pro.ganhos");
  const pro = await resolvePro(user.id);
  if (!pro) return null;
  if (!(await caixaOn())) return <p role="status" className="text-graphite">Os ganhos aparecem aqui quando o caixa passar para o CRM.</p>;
  const cfg = await getProAppSettings();
  return (
    <main className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <h1 className="font-display text-[24px]">Ganhos</h1>
      <EarningsView data={await getProEarnings(pro, new Date(), { showServiceValue: cfg.mostraValorServico })} />
    </main>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 2: O spec**

`e2e/profissional.spec.ts`:

```ts
import AxeBuilder from "@axe-core/playwright";
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";
import { e2eSql, login } from "./helpers";

const MAIARA = "maiara@e2e.local";
const PREFIXO = "+557199777";
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const bahiaDay = (ms: number) => new Date(ms - 3 * 3_600_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
/** Um horário de hoje que começou há 5 min (perto da meia-noite, 00:01 de hoje: nunca vira ontem). */
const startedToday = () => new Date(Math.max(Date.now() - 5 * 60_000, Date.parse(`${bahiaDay(Date.now())}T00:01:00-03:00`)));

test.describe("app da profissional (Lote 7A)", () => {
  test.skip(({ isMobile }) => !isMobile, "o app é do celular; a gestão abre o computador dentro do teste");
  let mine = "";

  test.beforeAll(async () => {
    await e2eSql(`insert into settings (key, value) values ('agenda_modo', '"dupla"'::jsonb) on conflict (key) do update set value = excluded.value`);
    const [pro] = await e2eSql("select id from professionals where name = 'MAIARA'");
    const [bia] = await e2eSql("insert into professionals (name) values ('BIA E2E') returning id");
    const [c1] = await e2eSql("insert into contacts (name, phone_e164) values ('Paula Profissional', $1) on conflict (phone_e164) do update set name = excluded.name returning id", [`${PREFIXO}0001`]);
    const [c2] = await e2eSql("insert into contacts (name, phone_e164) values ('Outra Cliente', $1) on conflict (phone_e164) do update set name = excluded.name returning id", [`${PREFIXO}0002`]);
    await e2eSql("update appointments set status = 'cancelado' where external_key like 'e2e:pro:%'");
    const at = startedToday().toISOString();
    [{ id: mine }] = await e2eSql(`insert into appointments (contact_id, client_name, professional_id, service_label, starts_at, duration_min, status, origin, external_key)
      values ($1, 'Paula Profissional', $2, 'Corte E2E', $3, 60, 'agendado', 'crm', 'e2e:pro:' || gen_random_uuid()) returning id`, [c1.id, pro.id, at]) as { id: string }[];
    await e2eSql(`insert into appointments (contact_id, client_name, professional_id, service_label, starts_at, duration_min, status, origin, external_key)
      values ($1, 'Outra Cliente', $2, 'Escova E2E', $3, 60, 'agendado', 'crm', 'e2e:pro:' || gen_random_uuid())`, [c2.id, bia.id, at]);
  });

  test("entra e cai em /pro; o Hoje mostra só os horários dela", async ({ page }) => {
    await login(page, MAIARA);
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/pro$/);
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toContainText("Maiara");
    await expect(page.getByText("Paula Profissional").first()).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("Outra Cliente")).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test("Chegou → Iniciar → Finalizar", async ({ page }) => {
    await login(page, MAIARA);
    await page.goto(`/pro/horario/${mine}`);
    const card = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Horário" });
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Chegou" }).click();
    await expect(card).toContainText("Chegou");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Iniciar" }).click();
    await expect(card).toContainText("Em atendimento");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Finalizar" }).click();
    await expect(card).toContainText("Finalizado");
    expect((await e2eSql("select status from appointments where id = $1", [mine]))[0].status).toBe("compareceu");
  });

  test("Vi minha agenda de amanhã", async ({ page }) => {
    await login(page, MAIARA);
    await page.goto(`/pro/dia/${bahiaDay(Date.now() + DAY)}`);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Vi minha agenda de amanhã" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText(/Você viu a agenda de amanhã às \d{2}:\d{2}/)).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("pede folga e a gestão aprova no computador", async ({ page, browser }) => {
    await login(page, MAIARA);
    await page.goto("/pro/folga");
    const dia = bahiaDay(Date.now() + 5 * DAY);
    await page.getByLabel("Dia", { exact: true }).fill(dia);
    await page.getByLabel("Até").fill(dia);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Pedir" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("Aguardando a recepção").first()).toBeVisible();
    const ctx = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 }, locale: "pt-BR", timezoneId: "America/Bahia" });
    const gestao = await ctx.newPage();
    try {
      await login(gestao, "gestao@e2e.local");
      await gestao.goto("/agenda?pedidos=folga");
      const sheet = gestao.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Pedidos de folga" });
      await expect(sheet.getByText("Maiara · Folga").first()).toBeVisible();
      await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Aprovar" }).first().click();
      await expect(gestao.getByText(/^Aprovado/)).toBeVisible();
    } finally { await ctx.close(); }
    await page.reload();
    await expect(page.getByText("Aprovada").first()).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("telas da equipe e mídia barradas no servidor", async ({ page }) => {
    await login(page, MAIARA);
    for (const path of ["/atendimento", "/contatos", "/funil", "/config/profissionais", "/agenda"]) {
      await page.goto(path);
      await expect(page, path).toHaveURL(/\/pro(\?|$)/);
    }
    expect((await page.request.get("/api/media/00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000")).status()).toBe(403);
    expect((await page.request.get("/api/relatorios/numeros?periodo=mes")).status()).toBe(403);
  });

  test("axe sem violações graves nas telas do app", async ({ page }) => {
    await login(page, MAIARA);
    for (const path of ["/pro", `/pro/dia/${bahiaDay(Date.now() + DAY)}`, "/pro/semana", "/pro/folga", "/pro/avisos", "/pro/instalar", `/pro/horario/${mine}`]) {
      await page.goto(path, { waitUntil: "load" });
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading").first()).toBeVisible();
      await page.waitForTimeout(800); // fim das animações de entrada
      const r = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).withTags(["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag22aa"]).analyze();
      expect(r.violations.filter((v) => ["serious", "critical"].includes(v.impact ?? "")).map((v) => v.id), path).toEqual([]);
    }
  });
});
```

## Lote 7B: Área da cliente (agendamento online construído e DESLIGADO)

Seção 9 da spec. A cliente confirma, pede para remarcar ou cancela por um link sem login. A atendente manda o link com um botão que **só preenche o texto**. O lembrete da véspera e o agendamento online ficam prontos e desligados.

```ts
} from "./settings";
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: Schema e migration**

No fim de `src/server/db/schema.ts`:

```ts
/* ───────────────────────── Lote 7B: área da cliente (migration 0029) ───────────────────────── */

/** Link da cliente: um por geração; vários da mesma visita podem valer ao mesmo tempo. O token nunca é guardado: só o hash (SHA-256). */
export const clientLinks = pgTable("client_links", {
  id: id(),
  tokenHash: text("token_hash").notNull(),
  visitId: uuid("visit_id").notNull().references(() => visits.id),
  /** Início da visita quando o link foi gerado ("Seu horário mudou" quando o início atual é outro). */
  issuedStartsAt: timestamp("issued_starts_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
  /** null = lembrete automático. */
  createdBy: uuid("created_by").references(() => users.id),
  /** conversa | agenda | lembrete_d1 */
  createdVia: text("created_via").notNull(),
  createdAt: createdAt(),
  expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
  revokedAt: timestamp("revoked_at", { withTimezone: true }),
  openCount: integer("open_count").notNull().default(0),
  firstOpenedAt: timestamp("first_opened_at", { withTimezone: true }),
  lastOpenedAt: timestamp("last_opened_at", { withTimezone: true }),
  /** confirmou | pediu_remarcar | cancelou */
  lastAction: text("last_action"),
  lastActionAt: timestamp("last_action_at", { withTimezone: true }),
}, (t) => [uniqueIndex("client_links_token_uq").on(t.tokenHash), index("client_links_visit_idx").on(t.visitId)]);

export type ClientLink = typeof clientLinks.$inferSelect;
```

No mesmo arquivo, no `$type` de `outbox.options` (linha 360), acrescente `visitStart?: string`. É só tipo (o campo é `jsonb`), não gera SQL, e a Tarefa 7B.7 usa:

```ts
  options: jsonb("options").$type<string[] | { values?: string[]; source?: string; awaiting?: string; scheduledAt?: string; stage?: string; messageId?: string; visitStart?: string } | null>(),
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: Configurações (`src/server/domain/client-link/settings.ts`)**

```ts
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db, type Tx } from "../../db";
import { auditLog, settings } from "../../db/schema";
import { normalizePhone } from "../phone";

/** Texto modelo do link (seção 9.4.10), editável em Configurações › Página da cliente. */
export const DEFAULT_LINK_TEXT = "Oi, {primeiro_nome}! Seu horário na Casa Fiaes: {data} às {hora} ({servicos}). Para confirmar ou remarcar, é só tocar aqui: {link}";
/** Texto do lembrete da véspera (DESLIGADO até a gestão ligar, com a confirmação de envio real). */
export const DEFAULT_REMINDER_TEXT = "Oi, {primeiro_nome}! Amanhã você tem horário na Casa Fiaes: {data} às {hora} ({servicos}). Para confirmar ou remarcar, é só tocar aqui: {link}";

export type CasaInfo = { endereco: string | null; whatsapp: string | null };
export type ClientLinkSettings = { cancelarAteHoras: number; texto: string };
export type LembreteD1 = { enabled: boolean; hora: string; texto: string };
export type OnlineBooking = { enabled: boolean; antecedenciaMin: number; diasAFrente: number; servicos: string[]; profissionais: string[] };

type Q = Tx | typeof db;
const read = async (q: Q, key: string) => (await q.select({ value: settings.value }).from(settings).where(eq(settings.key, key)))[0]?.value as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
const write = async (key: string, value: unknown, userId: string, action = "editou") => {
  await db.insert(settings).values({ key, value }).onConflictDoUpdate({ target: settings.key, set: { value, updatedAt: new Date() } });
  await db.insert(auditLog).values({ userId, action, entity: "settings", entityId: key, data: value as Record<string, unknown> });
};

const textSchema = z.string().trim().min(10, "Escreva o texto (pelo menos 10 caracteres).").max(600, "Use até 600 caracteres.");
export const casaInfoSchema = z.object({
  endereco: z.string().trim().max(200, "Use até 200 caracteres no endereço.").nullable().transform((s) => s || null),
  whatsapp: z.string().trim().max(30).nullable().transform((s, ctx) => {
    if (!s) return null;
    const p = normalizePhone(s);
    if (!p) { ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", message: "WhatsApp inválido. Use DDD + número." }); return z.NEVER; }
    return p;
  }),
});
export const clientLinkSchema = z.object({ cancelarAteHoras: z.number().int().min(0, "Use de 0 a 168 horas.").max(168, "Use de 0 a 168 horas."), texto: textSchema });
export const lembreteSchema = z.object({ enabled: z.boolean(), hora: z.string().regex(/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/, "Use a hora como 10:00."), texto: textSchema });
export const onlineSchema = z.object({
  enabled: z.boolean(), antecedenciaMin: z.number().int().min(0).max(1440), diasAFrente: z.number().int().min(1).max(90),
  servicos: z.array(z.string().uuid()).max(200), profissionais: z.array(z.string().uuid()).max(100),
});

/** Endereço e WhatsApp da Casa na página da cliente. Sem endereço, a página não mostra "Como chegar". */
export async function getCasaInfo(q: Q = db): Promise<CasaInfo> {
  const v = await read(q, "casa_info");
  return { endereco: typeof v?.endereco === "string" && v.endereco ? v.endereco : null, whatsapp: typeof v?.whatsapp === "string" && v.whatsapp ? v.whatsapp : null };
}
export async function getClientLinkSettings(q: Q = db): Promise<ClientLinkSettings> {
  const v = await read(q, "cliente_link");
  const h = Number(v?.cancelarAteHoras);
  return { cancelarAteHoras: v?.cancelarAteHoras != null && Number.isInteger(h) && h >= 0 && h <= 168 ? h : 24, texto: typeof v?.texto === "string" && v.texto.trim() ? v.texto : DEFAULT_LINK_TEXT };
}
export async function getLembreteD1(q: Q = db): Promise<LembreteD1> {
  const v = await read(q, "lembrete_d1");
  return { enabled: v?.enabled === true, hora: typeof v?.hora === "string" && /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(v.hora) ? v.hora : "10:00", texto: typeof v?.texto === "string" && v.texto.trim() ? v.texto : DEFAULT_REMINDER_TEXT };
}
export async function getOnlineBooking(q: Q = db): Promise<OnlineBooking> {
  const p = onlineSchema.safeParse(await read(q, "agendamento_online"));
  return p.success ? p.data : { enabled: false, antecedenciaMin: 60, diasAFrente: 40, servicos: [], profissionais: [] };
}

export async function saveCasaInfo(input: unknown, userId: string) { await write("casa_info", casaInfoSchema.parse(input), userId); }
export async function saveClientLinkSettings(input: unknown, userId: string) { await write("cliente_link", clientLinkSchema.parse(input), userId); }
/** Ligar o agendamento online abre a página /agendar para qualquer pessoa: fica registrado quem ligou. */
export async function saveOnlineBooking(input: unknown, userId: string) {
  const v = onlineSchema.parse(input);
  const before = await getOnlineBooking();
  await write("agendamento_online", v, userId, v.enabled !== before.enabled ? (v.enabled ? "ligou" : "desligou") : "editou");
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { changedNotice, hourText, linkExpiry, linkPageState, longDay, onlyFirstName, renderLinkText, servicesText, shortDay, waLink, whenLine, type LinkAppt } from "./client-link";

/** Instante de Salvador escrito à mão: o teste não depende do fuso do processo (rode também com TZ=UTC e TZ=Asia/Tokyo). */
const bahia = (day: string, hm: string) => new Date(`${day}T${hm}:00-03:00`);
const NOW = bahia("2026-10-14", "12:00");
const ap = (o: Partial<LinkAppt> = {}): LinkAppt => ({ startsAt: bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), durationMin: 80, status: "agendado", service: "Corte feminino", pro: "ANA", ...o });

describe("link da cliente (seção 9.4)", () => {
  it("validade: o menor entre o fim + 24 h e a geração + 60 dias", () => {
    expect(linkExpiry(bahia("2026-10-16", "11:20"), NOW).toISOString()).toBe(bahia("2026-10-17", "11:20").toISOString());
    expect(linkExpiry(bahia("2027-01-30", "11:20"), NOW).toISOString()).toBe(new Date(NOW.getTime() + 60 * 86_400_000).toISOString());
  });

  it("só o primeiro nome, nunca o sobrenome; datas e horas em Salvador", () => {
    expect(onlyFirstName("paula de souza")).toBe("Paula");
    expect(onlyFirstName(null)).toBeNull();
    expect(longDay(bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"))).toBe("sexta, 16 de outubro");
    expect(shortDay(bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"))).toBe("sexta, 16/10");
    expect(hourText(bahia("2026-10-16", "00:30"))).toBe("00:30");
  });

  it("agendado: os três botões; a visita inteira numa frase", () => {
    const st = linkPageState([ap(), ap({ startsAt: bahia("2026-10-16", "11:20"), durationMin: 40, service: "Escova", pro: "BIA" })], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), NOW, 24);
    expect(st).toMatchObject({ state: "agendado", canAct: true, canCancel: true, changed: false });
    expect(servicesText(st.items)).toBe("Corte feminino com a Ana e Escova com a Bia");
    expect(whenLine(st.start, st.end)).toBe("sexta, 16 de outubro · 10:00 às 12:00");
  });

  it("confirmado, cancelado (sem botões), já aconteceu e pedido pelo site", () => {
    expect(linkPageState([ap({ status: "confirmado" })], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), NOW, 24).state).toBe("confirmado");
    expect(linkPageState([ap({ status: "cancelado" })], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), NOW, 24)).toMatchObject({ state: "cancelado", canAct: false, canCancel: false });
    expect(linkPageState([ap()], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), 24)).toMatchObject({ state: "aconteceu", canAct: false });
    expect(linkPageState([ap({ status: "solicitado" })], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), NOW, 24)).toMatchObject({ state: "solicitado", canAct: false });
  });

  it("remarcado depois do link: avisa que mudou; uma das profissionais cancelou: mostra só o que continua", () => {
    expect(linkPageState([ap({ startsAt: bahia("2026-10-16", "14:00") })], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), NOW, 24).changed).toBe(true);
    expect(changedNotice(bahia("2026-10-16", "14:00"))).toBe("Seu horário mudou: agora é sexta, 16/10, às 14:00");
    const half = linkPageState([ap({ status: "cancelado" }), ap({ startsAt: bahia("2026-10-16", "11:20"), service: "Escova", pro: "BIA" })], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), NOW, 24);
    expect(half.items.map((i) => i.service)).toEqual(["Escova"]);
    expect(half.changed).toBe(true);
  });

  it("cancelar só até 24 h antes (fora do prazo, os outros botões continuam); sem duração não inventa o fim", () => {
    expect(linkPageState([ap()], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), bahia("2026-10-15", "10:30"), 24)).toMatchObject({ canAct: true, canCancel: false });
    expect(linkPageState([ap()], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), bahia("2026-10-15", "10:00"), 24).canCancel).toBe(true);
    const sem = linkPageState([ap({ durationMin: null })], bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), NOW, 24);
    expect(sem.end).toBeNull();
    expect(whenLine(sem.start, sem.end)).toBe("sexta, 16 de outubro · às 10:00");
  });

  it("texto modelo com as variáveis; sem {link} no modelo, o link vai no fim; sem nome, sem vírgula sobrando", () => {
    const v = { primeiro_nome: "Paula", data: "sexta, 16/10", hora: "10:00", servicos: "Corte feminino com a Ana", link: "https://crm.x/c/abc" };
    expect(renderLinkText("Oi, {primeiro_nome}! Seu horário na Casa Fiaes: {data} às {hora} ({servicos}). Para confirmar ou remarcar, é só tocar aqui: {link}", v))
      .toBe("Oi, Paula! Seu horário na Casa Fiaes: sexta, 16/10 às 10:00 (Corte feminino com a Ana). Para confirmar ou remarcar, é só tocar aqui: https://crm.x/c/abc");
    expect(renderLinkText("Oi, {primeiro_nome}!", v)).toBe("Oi, Paula!\nhttps://crm.x/c/abc");
    expect(renderLinkText("Oi, {primeiro_nome}! Seu horário: {data}. {link}", { ...v, primeiro_nome: "" })).toBe("Oi! Seu horário: sexta, 16/10. https://crm.x/c/abc");
  });

  it("Falar com a Casa: wa.me só com WhatsApp configurado", () => {
    expect(waLink("+55 71 99999-0000", "Oi!")).toBe("https://wa.me/5571999990000?text=Oi!");
    expect(waLink(null, "Oi")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildIcs, icsEscape, icsFold, icsUtc } from "./ics";

const bahia = (day: string, hm: string) => new Date(`${day}T${hm}:00-03:00`);

describe("arquivo de calendário (RFC 5545)", () => {
  it("16/10 às 23:30 em Salvador sai como DTSTART:20261017T023000Z (UTC), em qualquer fuso do processo", () => {
    expect(icsUtc(bahia("2026-10-16", "23:30"))).toBe("20261017T023000Z");
    const ics = buildIcs({ uid: "v1@casafiaes", start: bahia("2026-10-16", "23:30"), end: bahia("2026-10-17", "00:30"), summary: "Casa Fiaes · Corte com a Ana",
      location: "Rua A, 10; sala 2", description: `Para confirmar: https://crm.casafiaes.com.br/c/${"x".repeat(43)}`, now: new Date("2026-10-15T15:00:00Z") });
    expect(ics).toContain("\r\nDTSTART:20261017T023000Z\r\n");
    expect(ics).toContain("\r\nDTEND:20261017T033000Z\r\n");
    expect(ics).toContain("\r\nUID:v1@casafiaes\r\n");
    expect(ics).toContain("LOCATION:Rua A\\, 10\\; sala 2");
    expect(ics.startsWith("BEGIN:VCALENDAR\r\n")).toBe(true);
    expect(ics.endsWith("END:VCALENDAR\r\n")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("texto escapado e linhas de até 75 bytes, sem partir caractere", () => {
    expect(icsEscape("a\\b;c,d\ne")).toBe("a\\\\b\\;c\\,d\\ne");
    const fold = icsFold(`SUMMARY:${"ã".repeat(60)}`);
    expect(fold.split("\r\n").every((l) => Buffer.byteLength(l, "utf8") <= 75)).toBe(true);
    expect(fold.replace(/\r\n /g, "")).toBe(`SUMMARY:${"ã".repeat(60)}`);
  });

  it("sem fim conhecido, não inventa DTEND; sem endereço, sem LOCATION", () => {
    const ics = buildIcs({ uid: "v1@casafiaes", start: bahia("2026-10-16", "10:00"), end: null, summary: "Casa Fiaes · Corte", location: null, description: "x", now: new Date("2026-10-15T15:00:00Z") });
    expect(ics).not.toContain("DTEND");
    expect(ics).not.toContain("LOCATION");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: Implementar**

`src/lib/client-link.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Link da cliente (Lote 7B): validade, estado da página, textos e o modelo da mensagem. Puro (servidor e navegador).
 * Datas sempre no relógio de Salvador (Intl com timeZone "America/Bahia"), nunca no do aparelho.
 */
const TZ = "America/Bahia";
const HOUR_MS = 3_600_000;
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const LONG = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { timeZone: TZ, weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "long" });
const SHORT_DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { timeZone: TZ, weekday: "long", day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit" });
const HOUR = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { timeZone: TZ, hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", hourCycle: "h23" });

/** Status de horário que continua na visita (cancelado e lista de espera ficam de fora). */
export const LINK_ACTIVE = ["agendado", "confirmado", "chegou", "em_atendimento", "compareceu", "faltou", "solicitado"] as const;
const DONE = new Set(["chegou", "em_atendimento", "compareceu", "faltou"]);

/** Validade: o menor entre (fim do último serviço + 24 h) e (geração + 60 dias). */
export function linkExpiry(lastEnd: Date, now: Date): Date {
  return new Date(Math.min(lastEnd.getTime() + 24 * HOUR_MS, now.getTime() + 60 * DAY_MS));
}

/** "MAIARA" → "Maiara". */
const title = (s: string) => s.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/(^|\s)(\p{L})/gu, (_m, a: string, b: string) => a + b.toUpperCase());
/** Só o primeiro nome (LGPD: a página pública nunca mostra o sobrenome). */
export const onlyFirstName = (name: string | null | undefined) => {
  const f = name?.trim().split(/\s+/)[0];
  return f ? title(f) : null;
};

export const hourText = (d: Date) => HOUR.format(d);
/** "sexta, 16 de outubro". */
export const longDay = (d: Date) => LONG.format(d).replace("-feira", "");
/** "sexta, 16/10". */
export const shortDay = (d: Date) => SHORT_DAY.format(d).replace("-feira", "");

export type LinkAppt = { startsAt: Date; durationMin: number | null; status: string; service: string; pro: string | null };
export type LinkState = "agendado" | "confirmado" | "cancelado" | "aconteceu" | "solicitado";
export type LinkPage = {
  state: LinkState;
  /** Os serviços que continuam (todos, se a visita foi cancelada), em ordem. */
  items: { service: string; pro: string | null; start: Date; end: Date | null }[];
  start: Date;
  /** Fim do último serviço; null quando algum serviço não tem duração (nunca inventamos). */
  end: Date | null;
  /** O início mudou depois que o link foi gerado. */
  changed: boolean;
  /** Confirmar e "Preciso remarcar" valem. */
  canAct: boolean;
  /** Cancelar vale (dentro do prazo). Fora do prazo, a página mostra "Falar com a Casa". */
  canCancel: boolean;
};

/** O estado da página do link (seção 9.4.3). */
export function linkPageState(appts: LinkAppt[], issuedStartsAt: Date, now: Date, cancelarAteHoras: number): LinkPage {
  const sorted = [...appts].sort((a, b) => a.startsAt.getTime() - b.startsAt.getTime());
  const active = sorted.filter((a) => (LINK_ACTIVE as readonly string[]).includes(a.status));
  const shown = active.length ? active : sorted;
  const items = shown.map((a) => ({ service: a.service, pro: a.pro ? title(a.pro) : null, start: a.startsAt, end: a.durationMin ? new Date(a.startsAt.getTime() + a.durationMin * 60_000) : null }));
  const start = items[0]?.start ?? issuedStartsAt;
  const end = items.length && items.every((i) => i.end) ? new Date(Math.max(...items.map((i) => i.end!.getTime()))) : null;
  if (!active.length) return { state: "cancelado", items, start, end, changed: false, canAct: false, canCancel: false };
  const base = { items, start, end, changed: start.getTime() !== issuedStartsAt.getTime() };
  if (active.some((a) => a.status === "solicitado")) return { ...base, state: "solicitado", canAct: false, canCancel: false };
  if (now.getTime() >= start.getTime() || active.every((a) => DONE.has(a.status))) return { ...base, state: "aconteceu", canAct: false, canCancel: false };
  const state: LinkState = active.every((a) => a.status === "confirmado") ? "confirmado" : "agendado";
  return { ...base, state, canAct: true, canCancel: now.getTime() <= start.getTime() - cancelarAteHoras * HOUR_MS };
}

/** "Corte feminino com a Ana e Escova com a Bia". */
export function servicesText(items: { service: string; pro: string | null }[]): string {
  const parts = items.map((i) => (i.pro ? `${i.service} com a ${i.pro}` : i.service));
  return parts.length <= 1 ? parts.join("") : `${parts.slice(0, -1).join(", ")} e ${parts.at(-1)}`;
}

/** "sexta, 16 de outubro · 10:00 às 11:20" (sem duração: "às 10:00"). */
export function whenLine(start: Date, end: Date | null): string {
  return `${longDay(start)} · ${end ? `${hourText(start)} às ${hourText(end)}` : `às ${hourText(start)}`}`;
}

/** "Seu horário mudou: agora é sexta, 16/10, às 14:00". */
export const changedNotice = (start: Date) => `Seu horário mudou: agora é ${shortDay(start)}, às ${hourText(start)}`;

export type LinkTextVars = { primeiro_nome: string; data: string; hora: string; servicos: string; link: string };
/** O modelo da mensagem com as variáveis. Sem `{link}` no modelo, o link vai no fim: a mensagem nunca sai sem ele. */
export function renderLinkText(tpl: string, v: LinkTextVars): string {
  const text = tpl.replace(/\{(primeiro_nome|data|hora|servicos|link)\}/g, (_m, k: keyof LinkTextVars) => v[k]).replace(/,\s*!/g, "!").replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, " ").trim();
  return tpl.includes("{link}") ? text : `${text}\n${v.link}`;
}

/** wa.me da Casa com o texto pronto; sem WhatsApp configurado, null. */
export function waLink(whatsapp: string | null, text: string): string | null {
  const digits = whatsapp?.replace(/\D/g, "") ?? "";
  return digits.length >= 10 ? `https://wa.me/${digits}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}` : null;
}

/** "Como chegar": a busca do endereço no Maps (a página vai com Referrer-Policy: no-referrer, o token não vaza). */
export const mapsLink = (address: string) => `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(address)}`;
```

`src/lib/ics.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Arquivo de calendário (.ics) da visita, conforme a RFC 5545: horários em UTC (…Z), texto escapado, linhas de até 75 bytes
 * dobradas e CRLF. Nunca leva o nome da cliente. Puro.
 */
export type IcsEvent = { uid: string; start: Date; end: Date | null; summary: string; location: string | null; description: string; now: Date };

/** 2026-10-17T02:30:00.000Z → "20261017T023000Z". */
export const icsUtc = (d: Date) => d.toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, "").replace(/\.\d{3}/, "");

/** Escapa texto: barra invertida, ponto e vírgula, vírgula e quebra de linha. */
export const icsEscape = (s: string) => s.replace(/\\/g, "\\\\").replace(/;/g, "\\;").replace(/,/g, "\\,").replace(/\r?\n/g, "\\n");

/** Dobra a linha em pedaços de até 75 bytes (UTF-8), sem partir caractere; a continuação começa com um espaço. */
export function icsFold(line: string): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  let cur = "", bytes = 0;
  for (const ch of line) {
    const b = Buffer.byteLength(ch, "utf8");
    if (bytes + b > (out.length ? 74 : 75)) { out.push(cur); cur = ""; bytes = 0; }
    cur += ch; bytes += b;
  }
  out.push(cur);
  return out.join("\r\n ");
}

export function buildIcs(e: IcsEvent): string {
  const lines = [
    "BEGIN:VCALENDAR", "VERSION:2.0", "PRODID:-//Casa Fiaes//CRM//PT-BR", "CALSCALE:GREGORIAN", "METHOD:PUBLISH",
    "BEGIN:VEVENT",
    `UID:${e.uid}`,
    `DTSTAMP:${icsUtc(e.now)}`,
    `DTSTART:${icsUtc(e.start)}`,
    ...(e.end ? [`DTEND:${icsUtc(e.end)}`] : []),
    `SUMMARY:${icsEscape(e.summary)}`,
    ...(e.location ? [`LOCATION:${icsEscape(e.location)}`] : []),
    `DESCRIPTION:${icsEscape(e.description)}`,
    "END:VEVENT", "END:VCALENDAR",
  ];
  return lines.map(icsFold).join("\r\n") + "\r\n";
}
```

  - `liveLink(token, now): Promise<ClientLink | null>`;
  - `openClientLink(token, ctx: { ip: string; now?: Date; count?: boolean }): Promise<LinkOpen>`;
  - `icsForToken(token, ctx: { ip: string; now?: Date }): Promise<string | null>`.

```ts
    expect((await openClientLink(token, { ip: ipOf("10.1.0.10"), now: NOW })).kind).toBe("ok");
    expect((await openClientLink(token, { ip: "10.1.0.9", now: new Date(NOW.getTime() + 10 * 60_000 + 1) })).kind).toBe("ok");

    expect(await icsForToken(tokenOf(r.url), { ip: ipOf("10.1.0.11"), now: NOW })).toBeNull();
```

```ts
/** Já passou do limite? Só confere (não conta a tentativa). Para bloquear tudo de um IP que errou demais. */
export function blocked(key: string, max: number, windowMs = 10 * 60_000, now = Date.now()) {
  return (hits.get(key) ?? []).filter((t) => now - t < windowMs).length >= max;
}
```

`src/server/domain/client-link/links.ts`:

```ts
import { and, asc, eq, gt, inArray, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { appointments, auditLog, clientLinks, contacts, deals, lists, professionals, type ClientLink } from "../../db/schema";
import { hashToken, newToken } from "../../auth/tokens";
import { allow, blocked } from "../../auth/rate-limit";
import { env } from "../../env";
import { UserFacingError } from "../errors";
import { visitIdFor } from "../agenda/ports";
import {
  changedNotice, hourText, LINK_ACTIVE, linkExpiry, linkPageState, mapsLink, onlyFirstName, renderLinkText, servicesText, shortDay, waLink, whenLine,
  type LinkAppt, type LinkPage,
} from "@/lib/client-link";
import { buildIcs } from "@/lib/ics";
import { getCasaInfo, getClientLinkSettings } from "./settings";

export const BAD_OPEN_LIMIT = 30;
export const WINDOW_MS = 10 * 60_000;
const TOKEN_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/;

export type VisitAppt = LinkAppt & { id: string; contactId: string | null; name: string | null };
export type CreatedLink = { url: string; text: string; visitId: string; start: Date; expiresAt: Date };
export type LinkView = {
  firstName: string | null; state: LinkPage["state"]; services: string; when: string; changedNotice: string | null; canAct: boolean; canCancel: boolean;
  /** O início que a página mostrou: volta com cada ação, que é recusada se o horário mudou. */
  shownStart: string;
  address: string | null; mapsUrl: string | null; whatsappUrl: string | null; cancelarAteHoras: number;
};
export type LinkOpen = { kind: "limite" } | { kind: "invalido" } | { kind: "ok"; view: LinkView };

/** Os horários da visita (sem lista de espera e sem gêmeo), com a duração do horário (senão a do serviço) e o apelido da profissional. */
export async function visitAppointments(visitId: string): Promise<VisitAppt[]> {
  const rows = await db.select({
    id: appointments.id, startsAt: appointments.startsAt, durationMin: sql<number | null>`coalesce(${appointments.durationMin}, ${lists.durationMin})`,
    status: appointments.status, service: appointments.serviceLabel, pro: professionals.name, contactId: appointments.contactId,
    name: sql<string | null>`coalesce(${contacts.name}, ${appointments.clientName})`,
  }).from(appointments).leftJoin(professionals, eq(professionals.id, appointments.professionalId)).leftJoin(lists, eq(lists.id, appointments.serviceId))
    .leftJoin(contacts, eq(contacts.id, appointments.contactId))
    .where(and(eq(appointments.visitId, visitId), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf), sql`${appointments.status} not like 'espera%'`))
    .orderBy(asc(appointments.startsAt));
  return rows;
}

/** O horário futuro mais próximo (agendado ou confirmado) da cliente da conversa. */
async function nextAppointmentOfDeal(dealId: string, now: Date): Promise<string | null> {
  const [d] = await db.select({ contactId: deals.contactId }).from(deals).where(eq(deals.id, dealId));
  if (!d) return null;
  const [a] = await db.select({ id: appointments.id }).from(appointments)
    .where(and(eq(appointments.contactId, d.contactId), inArray(appointments.status, ["agendado", "confirmado"]), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf), gt(appointments.startsAt, now)))
    .orderBy(asc(appointments.startsAt)).limit(1);
  return a?.id ?? null;
}

/**
 * Gera o link da visita (gerar não é enviar: quem envia é a atendente). No banco vai só o hash do token. A validade é o menor entre
 * (fim do último serviço + 24 h) e (agora + 60 dias). Vários links da mesma visita podem valer ao mesmo tempo. O token nunca vai para log.
 */
export async function createClientLink(input: { appointmentId?: string; dealId?: string; via: "conversa" | "agenda" | "lembrete_d1"; actorId: string | null; template?: string; now?: Date }): Promise<CreatedLink> {
  const now = input.now ?? new Date();
  const appointmentId = input.appointmentId ?? (input.dealId ? await nextAppointmentOfDeal(input.dealId, now) : null);
  if (!appointmentId) throw new UserFacingError("Ela não tem horário marcado na agenda. Marque na Agenda para gerar o link.");
  const [a] = await db.select({ status: appointments.status, startsAt: appointments.startsAt, twin: appointments.avecTwinOf }).from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.id, appointmentId));
  if (!a || a.twin) throw new UserFacingError("Horário não encontrado.");
  if (a.status !== "agendado" && a.status !== "confirmado") throw new UserFacingError("Só dá para gerar o link de horário agendado ou confirmado.");
  if (a.startsAt.getTime() <= now.getTime()) throw new UserFacingError("Esse horário já passou.");
  const visitId = await visitIdFor(appointmentId);
  const appts = (await visitAppointments(visitId)).filter((x) => (LINK_ACTIVE as readonly string[]).includes(x.status));
  const page = linkPageState(appts, appts[0]?.startsAt ?? a.startsAt, now, 0);
  const lastEnd = new Date(Math.max(...appts.map((x) => x.startsAt.getTime() + (x.durationMin ?? 0) * 60_000)));
  const expiresAt = linkExpiry(lastEnd, now);
  const token = newToken();
  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    await tx.insert(clientLinks).values({ tokenHash: hashToken(token), visitId, issuedStartsAt: page.start, createdBy: input.actorId, createdVia: input.via, createdAt: now, expiresAt });
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: input.actorId, action: "gerou_link_cliente", entity: "visit", entityId: visitId, data: { via: input.via, hash: hashToken(token).slice(0, 8) } });
  });
  const url = `${env().APP_URL}/c/${token}`;
  const tpl = input.template ?? (await getClientLinkSettings()).texto;
  const text = renderLinkText(tpl, { primeiro_nome: onlyFirstName(appts[0]?.name) ?? "", data: shortDay(page.start), hora: hourText(page.start), servicos: servicesText(page.items), link: url });
  return { url, text, visitId, start: page.start, expiresAt };
}

/** O link vivo pelo token (no banco, só o hash). Formato errado nem consulta. Vencido ou revogado: null. */
export async function liveLink(token: string, now: Date): Promise<ClientLink | null> {
  if (!TOKEN_RE.test(token)) return null;
  const [l] = await db.select().from(clientLinks).where(eq(clientLinks.tokenHash, hashToken(token)));
  return l && !l.revokedAt && l.expiresAt.getTime() > now.getTime() ? l : null;
}

async function buildView(link: ClientLink, now: Date): Promise<LinkView> {
  const [appts, casa, cfg] = await Promise.all([visitAppointments(link.visitId), getCasaInfo(), getClientLinkSettings()]);
  const page = linkPageState(appts, link.issuedStartsAt, now, cfg.cancelarAteHoras);
  const first = onlyFirstName(appts[0]?.name);
  return {
    firstName: first, state: page.state, services: servicesText(page.items), when: whenLine(page.start, page.end),
    changedNotice: page.changed && page.state !== "cancelado" ? changedNotice(page.start) : null,
    canAct: page.canAct, canCancel: page.canCancel, shownStart: page.start.toISOString(),
    address: casa.endereco, mapsUrl: casa.endereco ? mapsLink(casa.endereco) : null,
    whatsappUrl: waLink(casa.whatsapp, `Oi! Sou ${first ?? "cliente da Casa"} e preciso falar sobre meu horário de ${shortDay(page.start)} às ${hourText(page.start)}.`),
    cancelarAteHoras: cfg.cancelarAteHoras,
  };
}

/**
 * Abre o link (página pública, sem login). Link errado, vencido ou revogado: a mesma página genérica, sem dizer qual dos dois.
 * Com 30 aberturas com link errado por IP em 10 min, tudo daquele IP vira "Muitas tentativas". Conta as aberturas.
 * Nunca devolve id interno nem dado além do primeiro nome.
 */
export async function openClientLink(token: string, ctx: { ip: string; now?: Date; count?: boolean }): Promise<LinkOpen> {
  const now = ctx.now ?? new Date();
  const badKey = `link-ruim:${ctx.ip}`;
  if (blocked(badKey, BAD_OPEN_LIMIT, WINDOW_MS, now.getTime())) return { kind: "limite" };
  const link = await liveLink(token, now);
  if (!link) { allow(badKey, BAD_OPEN_LIMIT, WINDOW_MS, now.getTime()); return { kind: "invalido" }; }
  if (ctx.count !== false) {
    await db.update(clientLinks).set({ openCount: sql`${clientLinks.openCount} + 1`, firstOpenedAt: sql`coalesce(${clientLinks.firstOpenedAt}, ${now.toISOString()}::timestamptz)`, lastOpenedAt: now })
      .where(eq(clientLinks.id, link.id));
  }
  return { kind: "ok", view: await buildView(link, now) };
}

/** O .ics da visita (seção 9.4.8): UID da visita, horários em UTC, sem o nome da cliente. Visita cancelada ou link inválido: null. */
export async function icsForToken(token: string, ctx: { ip: string; now?: Date }): Promise<string | null> {
  const now = ctx.now ?? new Date();
  const badKey = `link-ruim:${ctx.ip}`;
  if (blocked(badKey, BAD_OPEN_LIMIT, WINDOW_MS, now.getTime())) return null;
  const link = await liveLink(token, now);
  if (!link) { allow(badKey, BAD_OPEN_LIMIT, WINDOW_MS, now.getTime()); return null; }
  const [appts, casa] = await Promise.all([visitAppointments(link.visitId), getCasaInfo()]);
  const page = linkPageState(appts, link.issuedStartsAt, now, 0);
  if (page.state === "cancelado") return null;
  return buildIcs({ uid: `${link.visitId}@casafiaes`, start: page.start, end: page.end, summary: `Casa Fiaes · ${servicesText(page.items)}`, location: casa.endereco,
    description: `Para confirmar, remarcar ou cancelar: ${env().APP_URL}/c/${token}`, now });
}
```
